- **bespunky-browser-automation**: BeSpunky browser automation. `playwright` — drive a real browser headless (load, click, type, screenshot, scrape) for solo verification, bug repro, or repeatable scripts. `shared-browser` — one in-container Chromium the human watches and clicks over noVNC while Claude drives it over loopback CDP: co-debugging, live CSS/DOM verification with measured proof, pairing on a flow. Pre-installed and enabled in BeSpunky devcontainers. ([BeSpunky/claude-toolkit](https://github.com/BeSpunky/claude-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/browser-automation) · 2 skills, 1 hook file)
- **bespunky-communication**: How Claude speaks: ships the 'Pitch to the listener' output style, every answer pitched to the person reading it. Brief by default, for a reader who stepped out; full depth the moment they engage. One question per message, placed last; short reports of finished work that never drop a caveat; structure only where it earns its place; no unexplained jargon or bare verdicts. Presentation only. Default in BeSpunky projects; elsewhere pick it with /output-style. ([BeSpunky/claude-toolkit](https://github.com/BeSpunky/claude-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/communication))
- **bespunky-design-system**: BeSpunky design-system skills - styling as a SYSTEM on any stack (Angular is one adapter). design-system-first: the discipline - every visual value is a token from the design system, no raw hex, magic px or style reach-ins, and a pattern's second occurrence is promoted into the DS. design-tokens-and-theming: the techniques - token tiers, CSS custom properties as the runtime layer, a zero-output SASS API, light/dark/brand modes, encapsulation, and the DS library's entry points. ([BeSpunky/claude-toolkit](https://github.com/BeSpunky/claude-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/design-system) · 2 skills)
- **bespunky-engineering**: BeSpunky engineering skills, stack-agnostic. Foundation: architect-mentality (the mindset) and architecture-first (solve through design, not patches; fix the root cause). Technique routers: software-design, advanced-typescript, nx-monorepo-and-dx. Plus resumable-state (a deliberate home for view and working state) and typed-reactive-navigation (typed per-domain navigation, never a raw router call), each with an Angular adapter. Angular-only skills live in bespunky-angular. ([BeSpunky/claude-toolkit](https://github.com/BeSpunky/claude-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/engineering) · 7 skills)
- **bespunky-house**: The BeSpunky house standard for any repo, as a stack of detected layers on an Nx floor: the agent DX (composed devcontainer, Claude settings, window identity, HOUSE docs) by default; Node, JS libraries, the stack-free web dev loop, Angular, a design system, navigation, Firebase or Postgres only when worn. /bespunky-house:new creates a project, /bespunky-house:upgrade migrates one to the current standard, /bespunky-house:add-layer adds a layer. Generator-first; consented, never automatic. ([BeSpunky/claude-toolkit](https://github.com/BeSpunky/claude-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/house) · 2 skills, 2 commands, 1 hook file)
- **bespunky-product-ux**: BeSpunky product & UX design — a pipeline from messy request to built UI: distill-the-brief (the real problem), envision-the-experience (the felt world), stage-the-vision (bold web-native art), mock-to-choose (concepts judged by eye), realize-the-vision (the researched build). Beside it: astonishing-to-use (the co-equal UX force), redesign-means-rethink (redesign from a blank canvas), keep-users-oriented (waits and steps), model-intent-not-data (intent in, decisions out). - **bespunky-project-starter**: Scaffold new BeSpunky-standard projects, or retrofit the house tooling onto an EXISTING repo of any shape.
